Abstract: Structural probes train on Universal Dependencies (UD), which does not encode formal-syntactic abstractions such as phase boundaries or phase-internal cohesion. Whether large language models (LLMs) encode these remains an open question that UD-based probing cannot answer by construction. We evaluate structural probes on wh-movement stimuli where UD distances are invariant across conditions by design -- any non-zero effect therefore reflects structure beyond UD. The three conditions -- bare small clause, infinitival, and finite -- are ordered by the number of Minimalist Program (MP) phase boundaries the wh-element crosses.
  Across 13 LLMs from four families, we find a phase-count gradient on a cross-clause pair (12/13 models) and a 13/13 sign asymmetry on a within-clause pair whose UD distance is identical across conditions -- the latter specifically predicted by phase-internal cohesion, an MP abstraction invisible to UD by construction. Activation patching confirms the representations are causally active in 12/13 models. These findings suggest that distributional pretraining can induce representations aligned with formal-syntactic abstractions beyond the reach of annotation-based probing; UD-grounded probes provide a lower bound on syntactic encoding, not an upper bound.

</details>


### [112] [Vectors Are Not Neutral: Sensitive-Information Inference from Exported LLM Representations in Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26433)
*Weixin Liu,Bowen Qu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM摘要系统可能将私有输入的压缩向量表示传递给下游组件，即使源文档受限，这些向量仍存在信息泄露风险。本文以临床出院小结生成为案例，通过EHR记录的种族标签审计发现：减少某个导出向量中敏感标签的可恢复性，并不一定能减少另一个导出向量中的可恢复性。为此提出SurfaceLoRA方法，针对特定导出向量进行参数高效微调，可将其中的种族信息恢复率降至随机水平，但未针对的池化向量中恢复率仍较高。结论：隐私审计和缓解措施应针对实际暴露给下游组件的具体向量制品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM摘要系统会将私有输入的压缩向量表示传递给下游组件，这些向量可能在不同访问控制下被处理，仍支持敏感信息推断，存在残余信息泄露风险。需要研究这一风险并寻找缓解方法。

Method: 以临床出院小结生成为高利害案例，使用EHR记录的种族作为受控敏感标签审计两个可能被保留或暴露的向量制品（最终提示词隐藏状态和平均池化表示）。提出SurfaceLoRA方法，在导出向量上附加梯度反转鉴别器进行参数高效微调。

Result: 减少一个导出向量中敏感标签的可恢复性并不减少另一个向量中的可恢复性。SurfaceLoRA能将目标（最终token）向量中的种族恢复率降至随机水平，同时保持摘要效用，但未针对的池化向量中恢复率仍显著更高。

Conclusion: 隐私审计和缓解措施应针对实际暴露给下游组件的具体向量制品进行，不同向量制品间的隐私风险不可互换。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) summarization systems may pass compact vector representations of private inputs to downstream retrieval, monitoring, audit, or analytic workflows. Even when source documents remain access-restricted, derived vectors may be handled under different access controls and still support sensitive-information inference, creating a residual information-disclosure risk. We study this issue in clinical discharge-summary generation as a high-stakes case study, using electronic health record (EHR)-recorded race as a controlled sensitive-label audit. We audit two artifacts that a system might retain or expose to downstream components: the final prompt-token hidden state and the mean-pooled prompt representation. Our results show that reducing recoverability of the case-study sensitive label from one exported artifact does not necessarily reduce recoverability from another. As a mitigation case study, we introduce SurfaceLoRA, an exported-vector-targeted parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that uses a gradient-reversal discriminator attached to a designated exported vector. Under a balanced five-way probing protocol, SurfaceLoRA reduces EHR-recorded race recoverability from the targeted final-token artifact toward chance while preserving summarization utility, yet recoverability remains substantially higher from untargeted pooled artifacts. These findings show that privacy auditing and mitigation should be performed on the exact vector artifact retained or exposed to downstream components.

</details>


### [113] [Targeted Remasking: Replacing Token Editing with Token-to-Mask Refinement in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26436)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-to-Mask（T2M）重掩码机制，作为LLaDA等离散掩码扩散语言模型中T2T编辑的无训练替代方案。T2M将疑似错误token重置为掩码状态，通过三种错误检测策略（概率基、触发镜像、时序差分）净化生成上下文，在12个基准测试中平均提升性能，尤其是数学任务（CMATH提升5.92%），并能修复59.4%的"最后一英里token损坏"错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2T编辑存在三大局限性：错误检测与替换耦合、用潜在错误token污染生成上下文、训练-推理噪声不匹配（系统性模型生成错误不同于训练时的随机扰动）。

Method: 提出T2M重掩码，将疑似错误token重置为掩码状态；设计三种互补错误检测策略：概率基、触发镜像、时序差分；提供统一理论分析证明T2M能净化上下文、将推理错误转为模型原生掩码噪声、实现延迟承诺以联合多位置优化。

Result: 在12个基准测试中T2M普遍提升精确token级输出性能，CMATH数学任务提升最大（+5.92%）。错误分析表明主要失败模式为"最后一英里token损坏"，T2M修复其中59.4%的案例。

Conclusion: T2M重掩码作为无训练替代方案，有效克服T2T编辑的固有缺陷，尤其适用于需要精确token级输出的任务。

Abstract: Discrete masked diffusion language models such as LLaDA generate text through iterative denoising, where mask tokens are progressively replaced with predicted tokens. LLaDA2.1 introduced a Token-to-Token (T2T) editing mechanism that accelerates generation by directly replacing committed tokens suspected of being incorrect. However, we identify fundamental limitations of T2T editing: it couples error detection with replacement, pollutes the generation context with potentially incorrect tokens, and introduces a train-inference noise mismatch where systematic model-generated errors differ from the random perturbations seen during training. We propose Token-to-Mask (T2M) remasking, a training-free, drop-in replacement for T2T editing that resets suspected erroneous tokens back to the mask state, allowing the diffusion process to re-predict them under cleaner context. We design and empirically validate three complementary error detection strategies -- probability-based, trigger-mirrored, and temporal-difference-based -- and provide a unified theoretical analysis showing that T2M remasking purifies the generation context, converts systematic inference errors back to the model's native mask noise type, and enables delayed commitment for joint multi-position optimization. Comprehensive experiments across 12 benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, mathematics, coding, and instruction following show that T2M generally improves performance on tasks requiring precise token-level output, with the largest gain on mathematics (+5.92% on CMATH). Error analysis on CMATH reveals that the dominant failure mode is last-mile token corruption -- where correct reasoning produces a corrupted final answer -- and that T2M repairs 59.4% of such cases.

</details>


### [114] [Conv-to-Bench: Evaluating Language Models Via User-Assistant Dialogues In Code Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26440)
*Victor M. dos Santos,Andre C. Castro,Samuel L. de S. Toledo,Bruno M. L. Calura,Lisandra C. de M. Menezes,Raul C. R. Mata,Telma W. de L. Soares,Bryan L. M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Conv-to-Bench是一个多阶段框架，能够自动将真实的多轮用户-助手对话转换为结构化的、可验证的需求检查列表，为LLM评估提供可扩展且低成本的替代方案。在编程领域，其生成的评估集与人工标注标准（如BigCodeBench）达到近乎完美的相关性（Spearman ρ=1.000），且计算开销显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估基准严重依赖劳动密集型的人工专家 curation，其扩展速度无法跟上LLM的快速发展，存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出了Conv-to-Bench多阶段框架，利用真实对话日志中的"指令演化"，将碎片化的用户意图解构为合并的指令和二元评估标准，自动生成结构化、可验证的需求检查列表。

Result: 在编程领域的评估中，Conv-to-Bench生成的评估集与BigCodeBench等人类标注标准达到Spearman ρ=1.000的近乎完美相关性，计算开销显著更低；LLM-as-a-judge框架的主评估器与人工验证的真实标签达成κ=0.705的实质性一致性。

Conclusion: Conv-to-Bench提供了一种可扩展、成本高效的范式，能够在用户中心AI应用持续多样化的背景下维持高保真度的评估标准；消融实验表明，多轮交互虽能捕捉用户意图的迭代演化，但指令中心的提取方法提供了更稳健的基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has outpaced the scalability of traditional evaluation benchmarks, which remain heavily dependent on labor-intensive expert curation. We address this bottleneck with Conv-to-Bench, a multi-stage framework that automatically transforms authentic multi-turn user-assistant dialogues into structured, verifiable requirement checklists. By leveraging the "instructional evolution" found in real-world conversational logs, our approach deconstructs fragmented user intent into consolidated instructions and binary evaluation criteria. Applied to the programming domain, Conv-to-Bench produces evaluation sets that demonstrate near-perfect alignment with human-authored standards like BigCodeBench, achieving Spearman correlations of up to $ρ$ = 1.000 with significantly lower computational overhead. Validation of the LLM-as-a-judge framework further confirms its reliability, with the primary evaluator achieving substantial agreement with human-verified ground truth ($κ$ = 0.705). Our comprehensive ablation studies reveal that while multi-turn interactions capture the iterative evolution of user intent, instruction-centric extraction provides a more robust foundation. Ultimately, Conv-to-Bench provides a scalable, cost-effective paradigm for maintaining high-fidelity evaluation standards as user-centric AI applications continue to diversify.

</details>


### [115] [Alignment Tuning for Large Language Models: A Data-Centric Lens on Alignment Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26442)
*Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从数据中心的视角，将对齐微调重新定义为流水线设计问题，将数据构建分解为响应合成、偏好评估和偏好实例化三个阶段，并基于此框架统一分类现有对齐方法，总结设计权衡、失败模式与高层次原则，最后指出开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐微调文献大多围绕优化目标组织，而对齐数据的构建过程常被隐式处理。本文旨在通过数据中心视角，系统解构对齐数据构建流程，揭示设计选择对优化信号的影响。

Method: 采用综述方法，将对齐数据构建分解为三个相互作用的阶段（响应合成、偏好评估、偏好实例化），以此框架对现有对齐方法进行统一分类，识别重复出现的设计权衡与失败模式，并提炼高层次设计原则。

Result: 提出了对齐数据构建的三阶段统一框架，识别了跨方法的常见设计权衡与失败模式，提炼了影响优化信号的流水线设计原则，并指出了提示级对齐、智能体场景和动态目标对齐等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 数据中心视角有助于理解对齐微调的设计空间，三阶段框架为系统化分析和设计对齐数据流水线提供了结构化的方法论基础，未来需关注更复杂的对齐场景。

Abstract: Much of the alignment tuning literature is organized around optimization objectives, while the construction of alignment data is often treated implicitly. In this survey, we adopt a data centric perspective and reframe alignment tuning as a pipeline design problem. We decompose alignment data construction into three interacting stages, response synthesis, preference evaluation, and preference instantiation, and use this framework to organize existing alignment methods into a unified taxonomy. Through this lens, we identify recurring design trade-offs and failure modes observed across prior alignment methods, and distill a set of high level principles that clarify how pipeline design choices influence the resulting optimization signal. Finally, we outline open challenges for alignment data pipelines, including prompt-level alignment, agentic settings, and alignment under evolving objectives.

</details>


### [116] [MicroSpec: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Lightweight In-Context Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26444)
*Zhiyang Chen,Daliang Xu,Yinyuan Zhang,Chenghua Wang,Mengwei Xu,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MicroSpec是一种无需训练的词汇剪枝技术，通过构建上下文敏感的紧凑活动词汇表（平均低于3k token，比原始词汇减少40倍以上），利用语言生成的时间局部性，在不增加训练参数的情况下将推测解码中草稿模型的推理延迟平均降低51.6%，端到端加速比达1.12-1.32x，优于EAGLE-2等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常使用超过10万token的词汇表，导致推测解码中最后一层线性投影层成为计算瓶颈。现有词汇剪枝方法依赖于固定或粗粒度的子词汇表，仍需约3万个活跃token才能保持草稿模型质量，效率不足。

Method: 提出MicroSpec，一种无需训练的上下文敏感词汇剪枝技术：（1）利用语言生成的时间局部性，在每个解码步骤动态构建紧凑活动词汇表；（2）通过异步收集和GPU驻留状态管理减轻稀疏内存访问开销；（3）作为即插即用模块与现有推测解码方法集成。

Result: 平均词汇量从超过10万降至低于3k（减少40倍以上）；草稿模型推理延迟平均降低51.6%；在多个基准上相对于EAGLE-2实现1.12-1.32x端到端加速；超越更复杂的基于训练的剪枝基线。

Conclusion: MicroSpec通过无需训练、上下文感知的词汇剪枝显著降低推测解码的计算开销，在保持模型质量的同时实现显著的加速效果，是一种高效且通用的优化方案。

Abstract: Large language models typically employ vocabularies of over 100k tokens, which creates a major computational bottleneck at the final linear projection layer when performing speculative decoding. Current methods for vocabulary pruning depend on either fixed or coarse-grained sub-vocabularies, requiring around 30k active tokens to preserve the quality of the draft model. We introduce MicroSpec, a training-free technique that overcomes this limitation by building a compact, context-sensitive active vocabulary on the fly for every decoding step. Exploiting the natural temporal locality found in language generation, MicroSpec attains high token coverage while reducing the average vocabulary size by more than 40x (down to under 3k tokens), all without any additional trained parameters. To translate this high sparsity into actual speedups on contemporary hardware, we present a co-designed system and algorithm that mitigates the overhead of sparse memory accesses via asynchronous gathering and GPU-resident state management. Acting as a plug-and-play enhancement, MicroSpec reduces draft inference latency by 51.6% on average, achieving an end-to-end speedup of 1.12-1.32x relative to the leading speculative decoding approach EAGLE-2 on various benchmarks, while also surpassing more sophisticated training-based pruning baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Curation and Extraction of Drug-Related Entities from Reddit Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26445)
*Zewei Wang,Zihan Xu,Yishu Wei,Michael Chary,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReDose数据集，包含6,435条Reddit药物使用帖子，由毒理学家和医学生标注了药物、剂量和效果实体。基准测试显示BiomedBERT在药物识别上F1=0.843，Llama-3 70B优于GPT-4，但效果提取仍是挑战（GPT-4召回率仅0.41）。该数据集旨在从社交媒体中提取患者自述的用药信息，弥合临床认知与现实用药之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生主要从临床过量案例中了解非法药物，限制了其对真实世界用药模式的理解，而药物用户在线上分享第一手经验，提供了药物剂量和效果的信息，但两者之间存在信息鸿沟。

Method: 构建ReDose数据集（6,435条Reddit帖子），由认证毒理学家为主标注训练集和测试集，两名医学生辅助标注测试集，标注药物、剂量和效果三类实体；并采用基于BERT、大语言模型和检索增强生成模型进行基准测试。

Result: 共6,267条标注被基准测试；BiomedBERT在药物识别上F1=0.843；Llama-3 70B优于GPT-4（F1=0.79 vs 0.72）；效果提取最具挑战性，GPT-4召回率仅0.41。

Conclusion: ReDose通过捕捉患者自述叙事，推动了从社交媒体进行医学数据提取的发展，但效果实体提取仍是未来需要攻克的难点。

Abstract: Physicians learn primarily about illicit drugs from clinical overdose cases, limiting their understanding of real-world usage. Meanwhile, drug users share first-hand experiences online, offering insights into dosage and effects of drugs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReDose (REddit Drug DOSe and Effect), a dataset of 6,435 Reddit posts on substance use. A board-certified toxicologist primarily annotated both the training and test sets, while two medical science students contributed to the test set, labeling DRUG, DOSE, and EFFECT entities. We benchmarked 6,267 annotations using BERT-based, large language model (LLM)-based, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models. BiomedBERT achieved an F1-score of 0.843 for DRUG, while Llama-3 70B outperformed GPT-4 (F1 = 0.79 vs. 0.72). EFFECT extraction remains challenging, with GPT-4 achieving a recall of 0.41. ReDose captures patient-curated narratives to advance medical data extraction from social media.

</details>


### [118] [Model Unlearning Objectives Vary for Distinct Language Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26454)
*Berk Atil,Vipul Gupta,Rebecca J. Passonneau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型去学习应根据不同语言功能（如危险知识去学习和毒性去学习）设计不同的训练目标，并通过余弦元学习变体RMU（针对危险知识）和基于层特定探针方向的多层目标（针对毒性）在四个7-8B开源模型上取得良好效果，论证去学习应像后训练一样被视为一类多样化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练过程中会习得不期望的属性（如危险知识和有毒文本生成），现有去学习方法未针对不同语言功能进行区分设计，因此需要探索针对不同去学习目标（危险知识与毒性）的专门方法。

Method: 针对危险知识去学习：提出基于余弦相似度的元学习变体RMU；针对毒性去学习：提出基于层特定探针方向的多层目标函数。在四个7-8B开源模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 两种方法分别在危险知识和毒性去学习任务上均取得了强结果，且采用了截然不同的训练目标。

Conclusion: 去学习应像LLM后训练一样被视为一系列不同问题，不同去学习目标需要不同的方法设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) learn undesirable properties during pretraining, including dangerous knowledge and toxic text generation. Just as post-training uses different objectives to shape different behaviors, we argue that unlearning methods should be designed for the language function at issue. To study this, we consider two mechanistically distinct unlearning goals, dangerous-knowledge unlearning and toxicity unlearning. For dangerous knowledge, we introduce a cosine-based, meta-learned variant of RMU. For toxicity, we propose a multi-layer objective based on layer-specific probe directions. Across four open-source 7-8B models, our methods achieve strong results, based on distinct training objectives for the two types of unlearning. Overall, our results suggest that unlearning should be studied as a family of problems, analogous to the multiple types of LLM post-training.

</details>


### [119] [Towards Error-Free EHRs: Reasoning-Intensive Consistency Verification Between Clinical Notes and Structured Tables in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26463)
*Yeonsu Kwon,Jiho Kim,Junseong Choi,Paloma Rabaey,Minseo Kim,Sujeong Im,Jeewon Yang,Jun-Min Lee,Sangji Lee,Jiwon Kim,Hangyul Yoon,Hyunwook Kwon,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EHR-ReasonCon基准和EHR-Inspector框架，用于电子健康记录中非结构化临床笔记与结构化表格之间的一致性验证，解决了现有方法缺乏深层推理能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子健康记录中笔记-表格一致性验证工作主要依赖数值或简单事件的表层匹配，无法捕捉临床解释、事件关系和时间变化等真实EHR文档中的推理过程，存在明显不足。

Method: 构建EHR-ReasonCon基准（基于MIMIC-III、专家注释、8048个实体），提出EHR-Inspector框架（分割笔记、提取锚点实体和时间参考、使用表格探索工具验证一致性），采用LLM-as-a-judge评估指标。

Result: EHR-Inspector在多个模型骨干上取得了最先进性能，各组件有效性得到验证，并揭示了与人工验证的差异。

Conclusion: 基于推理的一致性验证方法能有效弥补表层匹配方法的不足，为临床决策支持提供了更可靠的笔记-表格一致性验证方案。

Abstract: Data consistency between unstructured clinical notes and structured tables in Electronic Health Records (EHRs) is essential for patient safety and clinical decision-making. However, existing work on note-table consistency verification mainly relies on surface-level matching of numeric values or simple events. Such approaches fail to capture the reasoning underlying real-world EHR documentation, including clinical interpretation, event relations, and temporal changes. To address this gap, we introduce EHR-ReasonCon, a reasoning-intensive benchmark for note-table consistency verification. Built on MIMIC-III with expert-guided annotations, it comprises 8,048 entities derived from clinical notes and provides high-quality ground-truth labels. The annotation protocol is supported by specialized table-exploration tools to ensure systematic evidence retrieval and reliable consistency assessment. We also propose EHR-Inspector, an LLM-based framework that segments notes, extracts anchor entities and temporal references, and uses table-exploration tools to verify consistency against structured tables. Evaluated using expert-validated LLM-as-a-judge metrics under harsh and lenient criteria, EHR-Inspector achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple model backbones. Analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of its components and highlight differences from human verification.

</details>


### [120] [FAB-Bench: A Framework for Adaptive RAG Benchmarking in Semiconductor Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26476)
*Jingbin Qian,Congwen Yi,Min Xia,Wen Wu,Jun Zhu,Jian Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FAB-Bench，一个针对半导体制造领域RAG系统的端到端自适应基准测试框架，定义了六项诊断指标，并在4K-32K上下文窗口下评估了检索与生成性能，揭示了三种上下文缩放行为并确认注意力稀释是长上下文性能下降的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直领域RAG系统评估面临领域复杂度高、上下文尺度多样、依赖昂贵且不可扩展的专家评估等问题，缺乏系统化、可量化的评估框架。

Method: 构建FAB-Bench框架，定义六项诊断指标（事实准确性、上下文利用率、完整性、检索相关性、技术深度、推理一致性），耦合检索器诊断与生成器推理分析，在4K-32K上下文窗口上评估。从1300+候选样本中精选200个查询-答案对，涵盖三种合成策略。在4个LLM和4个RAG框架上系统评估。

Result: 发现三种上下文缩放行为：对数增长、早期饱和和冷启动动力学；识别注意力稀释为极端上下文长度下性能下降的主因；跨框架验证确认了评估的可移植性。

Conclusion: FAB-Bench为半导体制造领域的RAG系统提供了可扩展、可移植的端到端评估方案，揭示了上下文缩放行为与性能退化机制，有助于指导RAG系统在实际垂直领域中的优化。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become critical for knowledge-intensive applications, yet evaluating its performance in vertical domains remains difficult due to domain complexity, diverse context scales, and heavy reliance on expert assessments that are costly, inconsistent, and non-scalable. We introduce FAB-Bench, an end-to-end framework for adaptive benchmarking of RAG systems in semiconductor manufacturing. FAB-Bench defines six diagnostic metrics measuring factual accuracy, contextual utilization, completeness, retrieval relevance, technical depth, and reasoning consistency. The framework couples retriever diagnostics with generator-level reasoning analysis across context windows of 4K-32K tokens, quantifying how retrieval precision and generative fidelity co-evolve as contextual scope expands. From over 1,300 generated candidates, we curated a high-quality benchmark of 200 query-answer pairs spanning three synthesis strategies: needle-in-haystack, intra-document multi-topic, and cross-document multi-hop. Systematic evaluation across four LLMs and four RAG frameworks reveals three distinct context-scaling behaviors: logarithmic growth, early saturation, and cold-start dynamics, and identifies attention dilution as the primary mechanism behind performance degradation at extreme context lengths. Cross-framework validation on three additional production RAG systems confirms evaluation portability.

</details>


### [121] [Elias in the Lighthouse, Again? Diagnosing Low Diversity in LLM Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26492)
*Sil Hamilton,David Mimno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM生成的故事中存在高度重复的词汇（如Elias、灯塔、钟表匠），这些词汇源自偏好数据而非预训练数据，展示了小数据集与对齐算法结合可能产生的巨大影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的故事多样性极低，本文旨在探究这一现象的原因及其背后的机制。

Method: 从四个当前模型中使用五个提示词采样共20,000个故事，分析高频词汇的分布、来源及其与预训练数据和偏好数据的关系。

Result: 11个词出现在88.3%的生成故事中，这些词在已发表文献和预训练数据中罕见，但在所有模型可能共享的偏好数据中常见。相较于包含版权角色或成人内容的微调后故事，这些“灯塔故事”频率反而较低。

Conclusion: 小规模偏好数据集与强对齐算法（如RLHF）的结合，可能对模型输出产生不成比例的巨大影响，导致生成内容同质化。

Abstract: LLM-generated stories are a popular use case, but they show very low variability. We sample 20,000 total stories from four current models using five prompts. We find that 11 words occur in 88.3% of generated stories, with little difference between models. These words include names (Elias, Mara, Elara), settings (lighthouses), and professions (clockmaker, librarian). These tokens do not often occur in published literature nor pre-training data, but they are found in preference data that is likely to have been used by all current models. Surprisingly, these "lighthouse" stories are infrequent when compared with the average post-training story, much of which contains references to copyrighted characters or adult content. This result demonstrates the potentially disproportionate impact of small datasets combined with powerful alignment algorithms.

</details>


### [122] [Conceptual Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26537)
*Zhejian Zhou,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念隐写术（conceptual steganography），通过在思维链中编码高层次推理模式而非具体词汇来传递信息，该方法比传统关键词隐写术更具鲁棒性，且不影响推理效用。同时，策略感知释义器能有效关闭该信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链隐写术主要基于词汇或词法空间，而内容保留释义器已被证明能有效防御这类方法。本文旨在探索更隐蔽的隐写通道——通过推理行为模式而非词汇选择来编码信息，并评估其对现有防御手段的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出概念隐写术，在思维链的每一步通过高层次推理行为模式（而非具体词汇选择）来传递信息。在四个模型家族和两个推理领域上进行实验，将其与标准关键词隐写方法对比，并测试策略感知释义器的防御效果。

Result: 概念隐写术在面对强释义防御时始终比标准关键词方法更鲁棒，且编码信息不影响思维链在推理过程中的效用。策略感知释义器可大幅削弱该信道。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了一种新型隐写风险，并展示了策略感知释义器作为有效防御手段的可能性，为保障LLM推理的可信度提出了新的挑战和建议。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) emit Chains-of-Thought (CoTs) that drive much of their capability. However, the same sequence that carries useful reasoning can also covertly convey messages: a misaligned model may embed covert information in its CoT that slips through human supervision, a form of steganography known as encoded reasoning. Prior LM steganography schemes operate in the token or lexical space, and a content-preserving paraphraser is the canonical and effective defense in recent work. We introduce conceptual steganography, in which each step of a CoT carries information through patterns of high-level reasoning behavior, rather than through lexical choice. Across four model families and two reasoning domains, this backdoor communication channel is shown to be consistently more robust to a strong paraphrase defense than standard keyword approaches, and the encoding of information into CoTs does not affect their utility in the reasoning process. Having raised awareness of this new risk, we then demonstrate that a strategy-aware paraphraser can close much of the channel, highlighting new challenges and recommended defenses for ensuring faithful LLM reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [123] [Reliable Extraction of Clinical Follow-Up Instructions: A Hybrid Neural-Symbolic Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26560)
*Michal Laufer,Yehudit Aperstein,Alexander Apartsin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合神经符号流水线，将基于BioBERT的实体抽取（动作与时间）与确定性日期算术相结合，在合成门诊病历数据集上，对（动作，日期）对的提取F1达到0.986-0.997，而纯生成式方法（GPT-4o-mini、LLaMA-3）的对级F1仅约0.51-0.57，显著优于直接生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门诊病历中的随访指令包含（动作，日期）对，提取这些信息对排程和审计至关重要。然而，生成式抽取器在隐含链接和日期算术的隐式解码中表现不佳，容易遗漏日期信息。因此，需要一个能够显式处理实体抽取与日期计算的混合方法。

Method: 定义了TestSpecification和TimeSpecification实体及ScheduledFor关系。使用BioBERT进行BIO序列标注，结合双仿射链接器进行实体关系抽取；动作通过28类动作本体进行规范化，日期则通过确定性规则归一化为天偏移量。在2000份合成门诊病历数据集上进行评估，采用动作不相交的划分（18个训练动作，6个OOV测试动作），与零样本GPT-4o-mini和LoRA微调LLaMA-3 8B基线对比，使用bootstrap 95%置信区间。

Result: 在259份笔记的seen和OOV划分上，混合流水线在测试-时间对F1上分别达到0.997和0.986，MAE为0.00天。基线模型的动作F1较高（LLaMA-3为0.992；GPT-4o-mini在seen上为0.963），但Pair F1仅0.51-0.57（LLaMA-3）和0.53（GPT-4o-mini），置信区间与混合方法无重叠。

Conclusion: 将学习得到的实体抽取与确定性日期算术分离的方法在基准测试上优于纯生成方法，能够泛化到未见过的动作，并暴露失败模式。下一步验证是迁移到真实EHR笔记，局限性部分提供了初步的现实性检查。

Abstract: Objective. Outpatient notes carry follow-up instructions pairing actions with future times ("MRI brain in two weeks"). Extracting (action, date) pairs supports scheduling and audit, but generative extractors miss the date because linking and arithmetic are implicit in decoding. We test a hybrid neural-symbolic pipeline against direct generation. Methods. We define TestSpecification and TimeSpecification entities and a ScheduledFor relation. BioBERT feeds BIO tagging and a biaffine linker; entities are canonicalized via a 28-action ontology and times normalized to day offsets deterministically. We evaluate on a 2,000-note synthetic outpatient corpus with action-disjoint splits (18 train, 6 OOV-test) against zero-shot GPT-4o-mini and LoRA-fine-tuned LLaMA-3 8B with note-level bootstrap 95% CIs. Results. On 259-note seen and OOV splits the hybrid pipeline achieves Test-Time Pair F1 of 0.997 and 0.986 with 0.00-day MAE. Baselines reach high action F1 (LLaMA-3 0.992; GPT-4o-mini 0.963 seen) but Pair F1 stays at 0.51-0.57 (LLaMA-3) and 0.53 (GPT-4o-mini), CIs non-overlapping with the hybrid. Conclusion. Separating learned entity extraction from deterministic date arithmetic outperforms generation on this benchmark, generalizes to held-out actions, and exposes failure modes. Transfer to real EHR notes is the next validation; a first-pass realism check is in Limitations.

</details>


### [124] [Hubness, Not Anisotropy, Drives Cross-Lingual Retrieval Asymmetry in Multilingual Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26575)
*Adib Sakhawat,Fardeen Sadab,Atik Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言嵌入模型中存在检索不对称问题，本研究通过实验证明造成该问题的根本原因是集束性（hubness）而非各向异性等其他几何病理，并提出用CSLS替代余弦相似度作为默认检索指标可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言嵌入模型假设跨语言检索是对称的（即A语言查询检索B语言译文，反之亦然），但实际中并不成立。作者旨在形式化这种失败并找出其主导因果机制。

Method: 使用包含6,518条英语、孟加拉语、印地语、阿拉伯语习语和谚语的平行语料，采用五种生产级编码器（Gemini、Mistral、OpenAI-L、OpenAI-S、Qwen）嵌入，通过互近邻互惠性指标量化失败程度。进行五项预注册实验，通过联合回归分析集束性、各向异性、质心漂移、幅度等几何病理对互惠性的影响，并测试CSLS分数校正和集束向量消融的效果。

Result: 集束质量在联合回归中主导互惠性（49.5%主导份额，是次优预测因子的1.68倍；偏R²=0.302，各向异性仅0.003）。CSLS校正可弥合63.5%的最差-最佳互惠性差距，平均模型内效应量比集束向量消融大130倍。消融对比表明：集束性是相似度度量的病理，而非个别集束向量的病理。

Conclusion: 集束性是导致多语言检索不对称的主导因素，各向异性与集束性在统计上可分离，解决了已知的各向异性-集束性悖论。建议将CSLS替换余弦相似度作为多语言嵌入管道的默认检索指标。

Abstract: Multilingual embedding models are deployed under the assumption that cross-lingual retrieval is symmetric: if a query in language A retrieves its translation in language B, the reverse should also hold. In practice it does not. Using a parallel corpus of 6,518 idiomatic and proverbial expressions in English, Bangla, Hindi, and Arabic, embedded by five production-grade encoders (Gemini, Mistral, OpenAI-L, OpenAI-S, Qwen), we formalise this failure as a deficit in mutual nearest-neighbour reciprocity and test a single mechanistic claim: among the geometric pathologies of multilingual spaces, hubness, not anisotropy, centroid drift, or magnitude, is the dominant causal driver. Across five pre-registered experiments with falsification conditions specified in advance, hub mass dominates a joint regression on reciprocity (49.5% dominance share, 1.68x the next predictor; partial R^2 = 0.302 versus 0.003 for anisotropy), while a hub-aware score correction (CSLS) closes 63.5% of the worst-to-best reciprocity gap and yields a mean within-model effect size 130x larger than surgical hub-vector ablation. The latter contrast pinpoints the mechanism: hubness is a pathology of the similarity metric, not of individual hub vectors. We resolve the well-known anisotropy-hubness paradox by showing the two are statistically dissociable, and we recommend replacing cosine similarity with CSLS as the default retrieval metric for multilingual embedding pipelines.

</details>


### [125] [LATTE: Forecasting Peer Anchored Preference Trajectories for Personalized LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26612)
*Jinze Li,Xiaoyan Yang,Shuo Yang,Jinfeng Xu,Yue Shen,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LATTE框架，通过追踪和预测用户相对于同辈锚定的偏好状态轨迹，实现基于冻结大语言模型的个性化生成，在Amazon Reviews 2023和MemoryCD数据集上优于检索、摘要记忆、静态潜变量和软提示压缩等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法将用户历史行为聚合为静态表征，混淆了稳定身份、近期漂移和物品内容，缺乏对用户偏好动态变化的时序建模能力。

Method: LATTE包含三个核心组件：(1) 同辈锚定状态提取——对每个历史会话减去时间掩蔽的同类用户基线，得到相对偏好状态；(2) 轻量序列预测器预测下一状态；(3) 状态到Token桥接将预测状态注入冻结的指令微调LLM。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews 2023上，LATTE的ROUGE-L达到0.259，优于静态潜变量（0.219）和最强潜变量压缩基线（0.245）。消融实验表明改进主要来自轨迹预测而非软提示接口本身。

Conclusion: 同辈锚定能消除共享物品变异性，时序预测在陈旧平均值与噪声近期状态之间取得平衡，LATTE有效捕获了用户偏好的动态演化。

Abstract: Personalized generation with frozen large language models requires a conditioning signal that is both compact and current. Existing personalization methods typically retrieve or summarize user histories in text, or compress them into static latent profiles and soft prompts. These approaches are efficient, but they treat a user's past behavior as an aggregate profile and therefore mix stable identity, recent drift, and item content in the same representation. We propose LAtent Trajectory Tracking and Extrapolation (LATTE), a framework that represents personalization as forecasting a peer anchored relative preference state. For each historical session, LATTE subtracts a time masked baseline formed from comparable users who responded to the same item, producing a state that measures how the target user differs from peers under a shared item context. A lightweight sequence predictor then forecasts the next state in this trajectory, and a State to Token Bridge injects the forecast into a frozen instruction tuned LLM through a single anchored soft token. We provide a latent factor analysis showing when peer anchoring cancels shared item variation and why temporal forecasting trades off stale averages against noisy recent states. Experiments on Amazon Reviews 2023 and MemoryCD show that LATTE consistently outperforms retrieval, summary memory, static latent profiles, difference aware latent profiles, and soft prompt compression baselines. On Amazon Reviews 2023, LATTE improves average ROUGE-L from 0.219 for a static latent profile and 0.245 for the strongest added latent compression baseline to 0.259. Additional pairwise comparisons and diagnostic analyses suggest that the improvement is mainly due to forecasting user-specific trajectory information, rather than merely adding a soft prompt interface.

</details>


### [126] [Granuscore: A Reference-Free Measure of Granularity for Text Analysis and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26620)
*Lukas Ellinger,Alexander Fichtl,Miriam Anschütz,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Granuscore，一种基于层次嵌入空间结构特性的无参考粒度度量方法，能够在无需参考文本的情况下衡量自然语言的粒度水平。在Granola-EQ数据集上可靠恢复层次顺序，捕捉话语语境中的粒度差异，并解释句子特异性中除长度外的非线性变化。应用于四个问答基准后，揭示了问题、正确答案和模型输出在粒度上的差异，为分析数据集难度和模型行为提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言在不同粒度层次上传达信息，但现有度量方法主要捕捉表面细节或句子特异性，缺乏能够有效衡量文本粒度的参考无关（reference-free）度量标准。

Method: 提出Granuscore，利用层次嵌入空间的结构特性，无需参考文本即可衡量文本粒度。通过在Granola-EQ数据集上验证层次顺序恢复能力，分析不同话语语境下的粒度差异，并探究粒度与句子特异性之间的非线性关系。最后将其应用于四个问答基准，分析问题、金标准答案和模型输出在不同回答结果中的粒度特征。

Result: Granuscore能可靠恢复Granola-EQ上的层次顺序；在不同话语语境下捕捉到预期的粒度差异；解释了句子特异性中除长度外的非线性变化；在四个问答基准上揭示了问题、答案和模型输出在粒度上的一致差异，为QA数据集难度刻画提供了新工具。

Conclusion: Granuscore是一种可扩展、广泛适用的文本粒度分析工具，为理解模型行为和数据集特征提供了原则性分析视角。

Abstract: Natural language conveys information at varying levels of granularity, from fine-grained references to broad descriptions. While granularity is fundamental to human communication, existing measures mostly capture surface detail or sentence specificity. We introduce Granuscore, a reference-free measure of granularity that leverages structural properties of a hierarchical embedding space. Granuscore reliably recovers hierarchical orderings on the Granola-EQ dataset and captures expected differences in granularity across discourse contexts. Across domains, we further show that Granuscore explains non-linear variation in sentence specificity beyond sentence length. Finally, we apply Granuscore to four question-answering benchmarks and analyze how granularity differs for questions, gold answers, and model outputs across response outcomes. The analysis reveals consistent differences in model behavior and provides a principled lens for characterizing the difficulty of QA datasets. Together, the results position Granuscore as a scalable, broadly applicable tool for analyzing granularity in text.

</details>


### [127] [Bounded Path Context: A Controlled Study of Visible Path History in LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26645)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出有界路径上下文（BPC）方法，在LLM-based KGQA中仅向路由提示暴露最近K跳路径而非完整路径。实验表明，K=1在WebQSP上F1达0.487（优于完整历史的0.472），且输入token减少9.7-12.1%，证明了路径序列化长度应作为可调接口变量而非默认假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-based KGQA中，一个常见但未经检验的默认做法是将完整部分路径序列化到每个路由提示中，即使控制器已经以精确符号状态维护了该路径。这种做法可能引入冗余信息甚至干扰，因此需要探究路径历史长度的最优设置。

Method: 提出Bounded Path Context (BPC)方法，解耦路径的两个角色：控制器在符号记忆中维护完整路径用于答案提取和审计，而关系选择提示仅暴露问题、当前实体、出边候选和最多最近K跳历史。在固定图邻域、波束预算、深度、解码和答案提取格式的条件下，对K进行受控扫掠实验。

Result: 在WebQSP和CWQ完整测试集上，K=1在WebQSP上达到0.487 F1（完整历史为0.472），K=0在CWQ上达到0.287（完整历史为0.274），同时输入token分别减少9.7%和12.1%。在4B规模下K=1仍是最强设置。71-84%的样本不受历史长度影响。

Conclusion: 路径序列化长度应被视为可调接口变量而非默认假设，短历史在多数情况下足以匹配或超越完整历史性能，且能显著降低token开销。

Abstract: LLM-based knowledge-graph question answering (KGQA) delegates graph traversal to language models, turning each question into a sequence of local relation-selection decisions repeated across beams and hops. A common but untested default is to serialize the complete partial path into every routing prompt, even though the controller already maintains this path as exact symbolic state. Bounded Path Context (BPC) decouples these two roles: the controller retains full paths in symbolic memory for answer extraction and audit, while the relation-selection prompt exposes only the question, the current entity, outgoing relation candidates, and at most the last K hops. A controlled sweep over K -- fixing graph neighborhoods, beam budget, depth, decoding, and answer-extraction format -- shows that bounded histories match or exceed full-history prompting on complete WebQSP and CWQ test sets with Qwen3.5-9B-AWQ: K=1 achieves 0.487 answer-set F1 on WebQSP versus 0.472 for full history, and K=0 reaches 0.287 on CWQ versus 0.274, with 9.7% and 12.1% fewer input tokens respectively. At the 4B scale, K=1 remains the strongest setting on both benchmarks. Per-example analysis reveals that 71-84% of examples are unaffected by history length, while the affected cases expose when prior hops disambiguate versus distract. These results suggest that path serialization length is better treated as a tunable interface variable than as a default assumption in LLM-based graph controllers.

</details>


### [128] [AI evaluation may bias perceptions: The importance of context in interpreting academic writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26662)
*Shang Wu,Randol Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，评估科学写作中AI使用程度的方法若忽略国家和领域间的语境差异，会产生系统性偏差。通过对比人类撰写与LLM改写的摘要，构建了AI相似度基准，发现统一基准会混淆AI文本与既有风格差异，在LLM出现前就造成误判；而采用国家-领域特定基准能更准确地衡量AI使用强度。2025年数据表明，统一基准会高估某些国家/领域的AI使用，低估其他。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI使用检测方法未考虑不同国家和学科领域在学术写作风格上的固有差异，导致评估结果可能存在系统性偏差，需要更精细的上下文感知测量方法。

Method: 利用Dimensions数据库的大规模期刊论文数据，基于人类撰写摘要与LLM改写摘要之间的差异构建AI相似度基准。对比了统一基准（pooled benchmark）与国家-领域特定基准（country-field-specific benchmark）在LLM出现前后（包括2025年数据）的表现差异。

Result: 统一基准会混淆既有风格差异与AI生成文本，即使在LLM出现前的论文中也产生显著扭曲；国家-领域特定基准能有效减少此类偏差。2025年数据表明，统一基准系统性地高估了某些国家和领域的AI使用，低估了另一些。

Conclusion: 准确且公平地评估科学中的AI使用，必须采用考虑语境差异的测量方法，避免统一基准带来的系统性误判。

Abstract: This paper examines how estimates of AI use in scientific writing can be biased when evaluation methods ignore contextual differences across countries and fields. Using large-scale data on journal publications from Dimensions, we construct AI-likeness benchmarks based on differences between human-written and LLM-rephrased abstracts. We show that a pooled benchmark may confound pre-existing stylistic variation with AI-generated text, producing substantial distortions across country-field groups even in pre-LLM publications. In contrast, country-field-specific benchmarks attenuate such distortions and provide a more credible baseline for comparison. Applying these methods to publications in 2025 reveals that the pooled benchmark systematically overestimates AI use in certain countries and fields while underestimating it in others. These findings highlight the importance of context-aware measurement for accurate and equitable evaluation of AI use in science.

</details>


### [129] [The Labyrinth and the Thread: Rethinking Regularizations in Sequential Knowledge Editing for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26670)
*Zheng Wang,Kaixuan Zhang,Wanfang Chen,Jingwen Zhang,Xiaonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过优化分析证明了顺序编辑与一次性编辑的形式等价性，揭示了稳定顺序编辑的关键在于正确累积编辑约束而非复杂的正则化策略，并扩展至冲突编辑场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序知识编辑方法依赖复杂的正则化或约束机制，但这些机制的必要性尚不明确。本文旨在系统性地探究有效且稳定的顺序编辑背后的核心机制。

Method: 首先分析AlphaEdit的经验成功，通过严格的优化分析建立一次性编辑与顺序编辑的形式等价性；然后将该等价性推广至更广泛的编辑目标类别，证明稳定性源于正确累积编辑约束而非专用正则化或零空间操作；最后扩展框架处理冲突编辑。

Result: 许多常用的正则化策略对于可靠的顺序更新并非必要；所提出的框架能够处理冲突编辑，确保在矛盾更新下的鲁棒性和一致性。

Conclusion: 本文为顺序编辑提供了核心理论洞见，指明了通往更简单、可解释且可靠的知识更新路径。

Abstract: Sequential editing of structured knowledge in large language models allows targeted factual updates without retraining, yet existing methods often rely on complex regularization or constraint mechanisms whose necessity remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate the mechanisms underlying effective and stable sequential editing. Specifically, we first analyze the empirical success of AlphaEdit and establish, via a rigorous optimization analysis, the formal equivalence between one-time and sequential editing. Building on this insight, we generalize the equivalence to a broader class of editing objectives, demonstrating that stability emerges naturally from properly accounting for accumulated editing constraints, rather than from specialized regularization or null-space operations. We empirically confirm that many commonly used regularization strategies are unnecessary for reliable sequential updates. Furthermore, we extend our framework to handle conflicting edits, ensuring robust and consistent behavior under contradictory updates. Ultimately, our work provides Ariadne's thread through the labyrinth of sequential editing, charting a path toward simpler, more interpretable, and dependable knowledge updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wangzzzzzzzz/OTE-SE-Alignment.

</details>


### [130] [NestedKV: Nested Memory Routing for Long-Context KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26678)
*Hong Chen,Xiang Liu,Yubo Gao,Yuxuan Fan,Bo Wang,Yuanlin Chu,Yuanguo Lin,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NestedKV提出了一种基于多时间尺度余弦异常评分和无训练外部学习器的KV缓存压缩方法，通过全局、块级和滑动窗口关键锚点实现高效的长上下文LLM推理，在多种基准测试上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练KV压缩方法通常仅依赖单一的注意力、时效性、层级分配或键独特性信号来排序token，这在全局独特、局部偶发或即时相关的上下文场景中表现脆弱，亟需一种更鲁棒的压缩策略。

Method: NestedKV维护全局、块级和滑动窗口三种关键锚点，通过多时间尺度余弦异常对token进行评分，并组合排序结果；采用无训练的外部学习器，包含头自适应混合和惊喜门控token路由，配合自适应每头预算，无需训练或修改LLM。

Result: 在RULER、LooGLE、LongBench、LongBench-E、InfiniteBench和MMLUP-Pro等基准上测试Qwen3和Llama3.2模型，NestedKV在保留缓存较小时表现最强；Qwen34B上，在r=0.75压缩率时相比KeyDiff在RULER提升19.10分、LongBench提升19.29分；r=0.95时LongBench得分为37.32 vs KeyDiff的17.55。

Conclusion: NestedKV通过多维度评分和自适应路由机制，在不训练或修改LLM的前提下，显著提升了KV缓存压缩效果，尤其在高压压缩场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Long-context language models are limited by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache. Existing training-free KV compression methods usually rank tokens by one importance signal -- attention, recency, layer-wise allocation, or key distinctiveness -- which becomes brittle when useful context is globally distinctive, locally episodic, or immediately relevant. We introduce NestedKV, a key-only KV cache compression method inspired by the Continuum Memory System in Nested Learning. NestedKV maintains global, block-level, and sliding-window key anchors, scores tokens by multi-time-scale cosine anomaly, and combines the resulting rankings with a training-free outer learner using head-adaptive mixing and surprise-gated token routing. The score is paired with adaptive per-head budgets and requires no training or LLM modification. Across RULER (4k--32k), LooGLE, LongBench, LongBench-E, InfiniteBench, and MMLU-Pro on Qwen3 and Llama-3.2 models, NestedKV is strongest when the retained cache is small. On Qwen3-4B, it improves over KeyDiff by up to 19.10 points on RULER and 19.29 on LongBench at $r=0.75$; at $r=0.95$, it retains 37.32 on LongBench versus 17.55 for KeyDiff.

</details>


### [131] [An In-Vitro Study on Cross-Lingual Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26683)
*Adrian Cosma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个受控的跨语言实验框架（两种程序生成语言）研究语言模型中的跨语言迁移机制。在700次控制实验中，发现迁移效果主要取决于分词器是否保留可复用的跨语言子结构，而非词汇相似度或分词平衡。较小词表通过分解共享片段提升迁移性能，较大词表则可能将词汇原子化。迁移呈现阶段性特征：语法和类型级能力先于掩码词汇泛化出现。分词桥接机制与掩码可达性高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语料中存在词汇重叠、形态差异、数据不平衡和分词策略等混杂因素，使得跨语言迁移难以在真实语料中独立研究。因此需要构建一个可控的体外实验框架，以解耦这些因素并系统性地分析迁移机制。

Method: 设计了两种程序生成语言，它们共享本体、类型化语法和组合结构，但表面实现不同。通过独立变化词汇距离、小语种比例、分词器训练机制和词表大小，在掩码小语种条件下评估迁移表现，共进行700次控制实验。

Result: 迁移效果主要由分词器是否保留可复用的跨语言子结构决定，而非分词平衡或原始词汇相似度。较小词表通过保持词汇可分解为共享片段来提升迁移性能；较大词表则可能使词汇变为语言特定原子。迁移呈现阶段性：语法和类型级能力先于掩码词汇泛化。分词桥强度与掩码可达性高度相关。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移的关键在于分词策略是否能提取跨语言共享的子结构（如形态片段），而非表层词汇相似性。研究揭示了迁移的阶段性特征，并提出了"分词桥"机制来解释跨语言迁移的内在机理。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer in language models is difficult to study in natural corpora because lexical overlap, morphology, data imbalance, and tokenization are entangled. We introduce an in-vitro framework with two procedurally generated languages that share the same ontology, typed grammar, and compositional structure, but differ in surface realization. This lets us independently vary lexical distance, minority-language proportion, tokenizer training regime, and vocabulary size, while evaluating transfer on a masked minority-language condition whose lexical forms are never observed during training. Across 700 controlled runs, we find that transfer is governed less by tokenizer balance or raw lexical similarity than by whether tokenization preserves reusable cross-lingual substructure. Smaller vocabularies often improve masked transfer by keeping words decomposable into shared fragments, whereas larger vocabularies can turn forms into language-specific atoms. We further show that transfer emerges as a staged process: grammatical and type-level competence precede masked lexical generalization. Finally, we attempt to explain this mechanism through tokenizer bridges and show that bridge strength correlates strongly with masked reachability.

</details>


### [132] [The Need for an External Observer Formalizing the Sufficiency Gap: A Mathematical Extension of Mixture Identifiability and Contextual Grounding in Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26711)
*Francesco Corielli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含确定性文本机制和随机潜在状态机制的二元混合过程，证明即使无限容量的序列预测器在观测前缀与错误潜在状态兼容时也会变得过度自信，这种熵差是未观测状态边缘化导致的"充分性差距"。通过引入具有保真度的辅助二进制信号（检索、工具使用、外部基础），推导出上下文主导阈值：当信号保真度超过文本后验权重时，纠正信号能逆转后验几率。该阈值可缩小但不能消除充分性差距，完全消除需要完整揭示潜在状态或等效验证机制。分析表明温度缩放无法恢复缺失上下文，基础机制需兼具信息性和可学习可用性，高风险领域需要结构解耦的观测器或验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在混合机制过程中，即使拥有理想预测能力的序列模型也会因未观测到的潜在状态而产生过度自信的问题，即"充分性差距"（sufficiency gap），并探索如何通过外部信号（如检索、工具使用、外部基础）来弥补这一差距。

Method: 构建二元混合机制过程（一个确定性文本机制+一个随机潜在状态机制），引入具有保真度γ∈[1/2,1]的辅助二进制信号，进行贝叶斯更新推导，建立上下文主导阈值（contextual dominance threshold），分析信号保真度与文本后验权重的关系。

Result: 推导出上下文主导阈值：纠正信号逆转后验几率的条件是保真度超过文本后验权重；该阈值可缩小但不能完全消除充分性差距；完全消除需要完整揭示潜在状态或等效验证机制。此外，温度缩放无法恢复缺失上下文，基础机制需兼具信息性和可学习可用性。

Conclusion: 序列模型在高风险领域中需要结构解耦的观测器或验证器来弥补充分性差距；温度缩放等技术手段无法替代真正的上下文基础；外部信号必须同时满足信息性和模型可学习可用性两个条件才能有效消除过度自信问题。

Abstract: We construct a binary mixed-regime process with one deterministic textual regime and one random regime governed by an unobserved latent state. Even an ideal infinite-capacity sequence predictor that exactly recovers the text-only marginal law can become overconfident when the observed prefix is compatible with the wrong latent regime. The resulting entropy difference is not an ordinary optimization error; it is a sufficiency gap caused by marginalization over an unobserved state. We then formalize retrieval, tool use, and external grounding through an auxiliary binary signal with fidelity $γ\in [1/2,1]$. The resulting Bayesian update yields a contextual dominance threshold: a corrective signal reverses the posterior odds induced by the textual history exactly when its fidelity exceeds the text-only posterior weight assigned to the misleading regime. This threshold reduces, but does not generally eliminate, the sufficiency gap; complete closure requires perfect revelation of the relevant latent state or an equivalent verification mechanism. The analysis clarifies why temperature scaling cannot restore missing context, why grounding mechanisms must be both informative and learnably usable by the model, and why autonomous sequence models require structurally decoupled observers or verifiers in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [133] [Rethinking the Multilingual Reasoning Gap with Layer Swap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26735)
*Maxence Lasbordes,Amélie Chatelain,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建六语言长思维链数据集，发现本地推理差距远小于先前报道（仅1.9-3.5%），并通过权重空间分析发现模型存在语言无关的推理核心与语言特定的外层，提出层交换方法（Layer Swap）将英语专家的中间层迁移到各本地专家，几乎消除了本地推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明强制模型用非英语语言进行思维链推理（本地推理）会显著降低性能，但这些研究大多依赖推理时干预或有限的本地语言训练数据。作者希望在更大规模、同等监督条件下重新审视这一本地推理差距。

Method: 构建六种语言的长链推理数据集，基于Qwen3-8B-Base微调本地推理和英语桥接推理的专家模型。通过权重空间分析揭示中间层与外围层的功能分化，提出Layer Swap方法将英语专家的中间推理层迁移到本地专家模型中。

Result: 在五种非英语语言上，本地推理差距缩小至1.9-3.5%，远小于先前报道。Layer Swap方法在保持目标语言CoT的同时，几乎消除了这一差距。

Conclusion: 大语言模型存在一个语言无关的推理核心（中间层）和语言特定的外围层，通过层交换可以有效弥合本地推理差距，实现多语言推理能力。

Abstract: Recent reasoning Large Language Models produce a chain-of-thought (CoT) predominantly in English, even when prompted in non-English languages. Prior work suggests that forcing the CoT to remain in the input language (\emph{native reasoning}) substantially degrades performance relative to allowing the model to reason in English before answering in the input language (\emph{English-pivoted reasoning}). However, most studies of this native reasoning gap rely on inference-time interventions or limited native-language training data. We revisit this comparison at a larger scale and under comparable supervision. We construct long multilingual reasoning datasets across six languages (English, French, German, Spanish, Chinese and Swahili); fine-tune specialists in both native and English-pivoted regimes on top of \texttt{Qwen/Qwen3-8B-Base}, and evaluate across mathematics, science, general knowledge, and code. In this setting, the average native reasoning gap shrinks to 1.9--3.5\% across the five non-English languages, considerably smaller than previously reported. Weight-space analysis of the native specialists reveals aligned fine-tuning updates in the middle layers and divergence in the outer layers. This points to a largely language-agnostic reasoning core surrounded by language-specific layers. Exploiting this structure, we introduce a Layer Swap: transferring the English specialist's stronger reasoning mid-layers into each native specialist, closing most of the native reasoning gap across the five non-English languages while preserving CoT in the target language. We release all models and datasets.

</details>


### [134] [From Snippets to Semantics: Rethinking Evidence Granularity for Multilingual Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26755)
*Babu Kumar,Gaurav Kumar,Ayush Garg,Aditya Kishore,Jasabanta Patro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SEEK框架，通过自适应语义块提取（基于主题转换检测）从完整事实核查文章中构建连贯证据块，结合多语言编码器和LoRA微调的多语言大模型，在多语言事实验证任务中显著优于传统分块方法（F1提升10-20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实验证系统依赖搜索片段、句子级别证据或局部段落分割，导致证据碎片化且缺失关键上下文，限制了多语言事实验证的可靠性。

Method: 提出SEEK框架：1) 基于语义主题转换检测的自适应分块策略，从完整事实核查文章中构建连贯证据块；2) 使用多语言编码器对构建的块进行编码；3) 采用LoRA适配器微调多语言大模型进行真实性预测。

Result: 在X-FACT和RU22Fact数据集上，SEEK相比语义分块提升10% macro-F1，相比句子分块提升19%，相比搜索片段基线提升20%。证据完整性和显著性分析进一步验证了SEEK保留了更丰富的验证上下文。

Conclusion: 通过构建语义连贯的证据块而非碎片化文本，SEEK显著提升了多语言事实验证的可靠性，证明了证据完整性和上下文保留对事实预测的重要性。

Abstract: Multilingual fact verification requires evidence that is both relevant and sufficiently complete for reliable factuality prediction. However, existing systems often rely on search snippets, sentence-level evidence, or locally segmented passages, which can miss decisive context and produce fragmented evidence. To overcome these limitations, we propose SEEK, a Semantic Evidence Extraction with an adaptive chunKing framework that constructs coherent evidence chunks from full fact-checking articles by identifying semantic topic transitions and preserving local verification context. The constructed chunks are encoded using a multilingual encoder and then multilingual LLMs are finetuned using LoRA adapter for veracity prediction. Experiments on X-FACT and RU22Fact show that SEEK improves macro-f1 by up to 10% over semantic chunking, 19% over sentence chunking, and 20% over search-snippet baselines. Evidence completeness and significance analyses further show that SEEK preserves richer verification context and enables more reliable multilingual fact-checking.

</details>


### [135] [Quality Without Usefulness: LLM-Generated XAI Narratives as Trust Heuristics Rather Than Decision Aids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26770)
*Fabian Lukassen,Jan Herrmann,Christoph Weisser,Alexander Silbersdorff,Benjamin Saefken,Thomas Kneib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLMs将XAI输出转换为自然语言解释(NLEs)的质量与实用性的关系，发现高质量NLEs在五类任务中均未提升任务准确率，反而膨胀了用户自信并降低异常检测能力，揭示了"质量-实用性差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLMs能生成高质量的NLEs，但解释质量是否转化为实际有用性尚未明确，作者希望系统性地探究这一差距。

Method: 在时间序列能源预测领域设计五项对照实验（共2,730次判断，60个测试实例），每项实验操作XAI文献中的不同实用性维度，保持NLE质量恒定在先前因子研究的高水平，同时引入安慰剂对照。

Result: NLEs在全部五项任务中均未提升任务准确率，但膨胀了被试的自信水平；安慰剂对照表明自信提升源于文本存在而非内容本身；在分布外检测任务中，NLEs反而降低了LLM法官识别不可靠预测的能力。

Conclusion: 发现了"质量-实用性差距"，主张对XAI-to-NLE管线的评估必须从单纯文本质量指标扩展到下游任务性能评估。

Abstract: Prior work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can transform Explainable AI (XAI) outputs into Natural Language Explanations (NLEs) that score highly on quality metrics such as plausibility, coherence, and comprehensibility. But does explanation quality translate to practical usefulness? We investigate this question in a time-series energy forecasting domain through five controlled experiments (2,730 judgments across 60 test instances), each operationalising a distinct facet of usefulness studied in the XAI literature. Holding NLE quality constant at the high levels established by a prior factorial study, we find that NLEs do not improve task accuracy on any of the five tasks, while inflating self-reported confidence. A placebic control shows that this confidence boost is driven by text presence rather than content. In an out-of-distribution detection task, NLEs reduce the LLM judge's ability to flag unreliable predictions, providing false reassurance that masks model failure. We characterise these findings as the Quality-Usefulness Gap and argue that evaluation of the XAI-to-NLE pipeline must extend beyond text-quality metrics to downstream task performance.

</details>


### [136] [EmoDistill: Offline Emotion Skill Distillation for Language Model Agents in Adversarial Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26785)
*Yunbo Long,Haolang Zhao,Lukas Beckenbauer,Liming Xu,Alexandra Brintrup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmoDistill 框架，通过将情感策略分解为情感选择（IQL）和情感表达（LoRA+ SFT/JPO），在离线对抗性谈判中蒸馏情感谈判技能，使小语言模型在多个高价值谈判领域超越大模型基线，实现高效的情感驱动谈判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练 LLM 的对齐优化使其变得安全礼貌，但在对抗性谈判中，这种对齐反而成为弱点：情感化语言可能使智能体让步于对方利益。现有工作忽略了情感作为战略性行动渠道的潜力，因此需要一种系统性方法来蒸馏情感谈判技能。

Method: EmoDistill 框架将情感策略分解为：(1) 情感选择——使用隐式 Q 学习（IQL）选择器学习何时表达何种情感；(2) 情感表达——通过 LoRA 基策略结合监督微调（SFT）和评判策略优化（JPO）学习如何表达该情感。整个流程在离线智能体交互数据上训练，避免在线谈判成本。

Result: 在四个情感敏感的高风险谈判领域中，EmoDistill 训练的小语言模型（SLM）策略在所有场景中取得最高效用，超越了普通 SLM/LLM 基线和仅使用 IQL 情感选择的变体。消融实验证实情感条件化至关重要，迁移研究表明模型能泛化到新领域、未见对手以及训练过智能体间的锦标赛场景。

Conclusion: EmoDistill 证明情感是对抗性谈判中的战略性行动通道，通过离线蒸馏方式即可掌握有效的情感谈判技能，无需昂贵的在线交互训练，同时具备良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-trained LLMs are often optimized to align responses with human preferences, making them safe, polite, and conversationally appropriate. In adversarial negotiation, however, this alignment can become a vulnerability: emotionally framed language may steer agents toward the counterparty's interests. Using GoEmotions-based affective prompting, we show that emotion substantially shifts negotiation outcomes, suggesting that emotion is a strategic action channel rather than a surface style. Thus, we introduce \textbf{EmoDistill}, an offline framework for distilling emotional negotiation skills into language model agents. EmoDistill decomposes emotional strategy into emotion selection and emotion expression: an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) selector learns \emph{which} emotion to express, while a Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)-based policy learns \emph{how} to express it through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Judge Policy Optimization (JPO). Across four emotion-sensitive, high-stakes negotiation domains, SLM policies trained under the EmoDistill framework achieve the highest utility, outperforming vanilla SLM/LLM baselines and IQL-only emotion selection. Ablations show that emotion conditioning is essential, and transfer studies demonstrate generalization across domains, unseen counterparties, and trained-vs-trained tournaments. Overall, EmoDistill learns skills from offline agent-to-agent interactions, avoiding costly online negotiation during training.

</details>


### [137] [SeDT: Sentence-Transformer Decision-Transformer Conditioning for Multi-Turn Conversation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26788)
*Ramakrishna Vamsi Setti,Jagadeesh Rachapudi,Sachin Chaudhary,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在单轮任务中表现优异，但跨多轮时性能下降高达39%（称为"Lost in Conversation"）。根本原因是扁平对话历史赋予每轮同等权重。本文提出SeDT（Sentence-transformer Decision-Transformer），一种无需训练、无需权重修改的推理时方法，通过引入离线强化学习中的return-to-go条件化，为每轮对话标注累积相关性分数（结合语义、词汇、位置信号），在最终轮展示完整标注历史。在三个LLM和三个生成任务的基准测试中，SeDT在所有9个模型-任务组合上均优于基线，平均性能提升最高达37.7%，同时7/9组合的不稳定性降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多轮对话中性能显著下降（最高39%），且这种下降主要是可靠性问题（不稳定性增加112%），根本原因在于扁平化对话历史无法区分关键约束与无关对话。

Method: SeDT（Sentence-transformer Decision-Transformer）：一种无需训练的推理时方法，借鉴离线强化学习中的return-to-go条件化，为每轮对话分片计算三种互补信号（语义、词汇、位置）的累积相关性分数，并将完整标注历史在最终轮呈现给模型。

Result: 在三个LLM和三个生成任务的Lost-in-Conversation基准上，SeDT在所有9个模型-任务组合中均优于分片基线，平均性能提升最高达+37.7%，其中7/9组合的不稳定性同时降低。

Conclusion: 告知模型哪些历史对话轮次是重要的，足以大幅恢复多轮对话中丢失的性能。SeDT的无训练、无权重修改特性使其具有实用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve impressive performance when a task is fully specified in a single turn, yet the same models lose up to 39% of that performance when the identical task is revealed incrementally across multiple turns, a phenomenon documented at scale as Lost in Conversation. Crucially, this collapse is almost entirely a reliability failure; the best case, the aptitude only falls 16%, while the unreliability more than doubles (+112%). We argue that the root cause is structural, a flat conversation history assigns equal implicit weight to every prior turn, giving the model no signal to distinguish a critical constraint from incidental dialog. We present SeDT Sentence-transformer Decision-Transformer, a training-free inference-time method that resolves this by importing return-to-go conditioning from offline reinforcement learning. SeDT annotates each conversation shard with a cumulative relevance score derived from three complementary semantic, lexical, and positional signals and presents the full annotated history to the model at the final turn, without weight changes, without training data, and without discarding context. Evaluated on the Lost-in-Conversation benchmark in three LLMs and three generation tasks, SeDT outperforms the sharded baseline in all nine model-task combinations, with gains up to +37.7% in mean performance P and simultaneous reductions in unreliability in seven of the nine combinations. In short, telling the model which past turns matter is sufficient to substantially recover the performance lost in conversation.

</details>


### [138] [Psychological Constructs in Shared Semantic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26801)
*Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，通过将心理构念表示为共享词嵌入空间中的方向，使不同测量工具、数据集和研究传统中的心理构念在语义上可通约，并以情感维度（VAD）为测试案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理构念通常在不同测量工具、数据集和研究传统中被测量，导致直接比较困难，亟需一种使这些构念在语义上可通约的框架。

Method: 使用监督语义微分法从文本-结果关联中估计构念特定的语义梯度，并将其投影到理论驱动的参考轴上；以效价、唤醒度和支配度（VAD）作为情感坐标系统，分别对GoEmotions的27种情感类别和大五人格域及子维度进行投影分析。

Result: 从英语词级情感规范中恢复了可解释的VAD方向；27种GoEmotions情感类别的语义梯度投影恢复了预期的情感组织，尤其在效价和唤醒度维度上；大五人格域层面的定位总体一致，而子维度层面的结果因问卷文本稀疏而更具探索性。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间能够支持原本不可通约的心理测量之间的构念层面比较，前提是需评估语义定位的稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Psychological constructs are often measured in separate instruments, datasets, and research traditions, which makes direct comparison difficult. This paper proposes a framework for making such constructs semantically commensurate by representing and comparing them as directions in a shared word-embedding space. Using Supervised Semantic Differential, we estimate construct-specific semantic gradients from text-outcome associations and project them onto theoretically motivated reference axes. As an initial test case, we use Valence, Arousal, and Dominance (VAD) as an affective coordinate system. First, we recover interpretable VAD directions from English word-level affective norms. Second, we project semantic gradients for 27 GoEmotions categories into this space and recover the expected organization of emotions, especially along valence and arousal. Third, we apply the same procedure to Big Five personality domains and facets derived from IPIP-NEO-300 item-factor associations. Domain-level placements are broadly coherent, while facet-level results are more exploratory because they rely on sparse questionnaire text. The results suggest that embedding spaces can support construct-level comparison across otherwise incommensurable psychological measurements, provided that semantic placements are assessed for stability and interpretability.

</details>


### [139] [Generating Logically Consistent Synthetic Supply Chain Data with LLM-Driven Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26823)
*Yunbo Long,Ge Zheng,Liming Xu,Alexandra Brintrup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabKG框架，通过构建列关系知识图谱(CR-KG)来捕捉供应链数据中的操作逻辑约束，并利用多LLM集成验证关系，结合潜在扩散模型生成逻辑一致的合成表格数据，解决了现有生成模型忽略操作约束的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在供应链分析中面临两个障碍：数据稀缺性和数据隐私。现有表格生成模型主要优化分布保真度和下游预测效用，生成的记录虽然统计上真实但违反基本的操作约束（如时间顺序、数学依赖、层次分类、条件规则），无法支撑运营仿真和决策。

Method: TabKG框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 构建列关系知识图谱(CR-KG)，利用多LLM集成和多数投票从列元数据中提出候选关系；(2) 根据真实数据验证这些关系，去除幻觉化或无法支持的边；(3) 将原始表格压缩为独立列，使用潜在扩散模型生成独立列，再根据验证后的关系确定性重构依赖列，从而在构造上强制执行逻辑一致性。

Result: 框架能够生成既在统计分布上真实、又在操作逻辑上一致的合成供应链表格数据，通过知识图谱引导的方式确保生成记录满足时间顺序、数学依赖、层次分类和条件规则等操作约束。

Conclusion: TabKG通过知识图谱引导的生成范式，有效弥补了现有表格生成模型在操作逻辑一致性方面的不足，为供应链分析中数据稀缺和隐私问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Synthetic data offers a promising solution to two persistent barriers in supply chain analytics: data scarcity and data privacy. However, for synthetic data to support operational simulation and decision-making, it must do more than reproduce the statistical distributions of real records, and also preserve the \emph{operational logic} that governs supply chain processes, including the temporal orderings, mathematical dependencies, hierarchical taxonomies, and conditional rules that make a record operationally plausible. We consider this logic as the ``physics'' of supply chain data. Existing tabular generative models are primarily optimized for distributional fidelity and downstream predictive utility, and therefore often generate records that appear statistically realistic but violate fundamental operational constraints. This paper introduces \textbf{\textit{TabKG}}, a knowledge-graph-guided framework for logically consistent synthetic supply chain tabular data generation. TabKG constructs a \textbf{\textit{Column Relationship Knowledge Graph (CR-KG)}} to represent data operational dependencies. It uses a multi-LLM ensemble with majority voting to propose candidate relationships from column metadata, validates these relationships against real data to remove hallucinated or unsupported edges, and then uses the validated CR-KG to guide generation. Specifically, TabKG compresses the original table into independent columns, generates these columns using a latent diffusion model, and deterministically reconstructs dependent columns according to the validated relationships, enforcing logical consistency by construction with respect to the discovered operational rules.

</details>


### [140] [ContextGuard: Structured Self-Auditing for Context Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26827)
*Hongbo Jin,Chi Wang,Haoran Tang,Zhongjing Du,Xu Jiang,Jingqi Tian,Qiaoman Zhang,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 尽管LLM具备强大的推理能力，但在复杂上下文知识的忠实应用上仍存在困难，尤其是在处理非核心、持续性或格式敏感的要求时会失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM在复杂上下文知识应用中的失败模式，说明这些失败并非整体性推理崩溃，而是对特定类型要求的遗漏。

Method: 通过基准测试分析LLM在上下文丰富任务中的表现，特别关注其遵循中心推理路径但遗漏外围、持续性和格式敏感要求的行为。

Result: LLM在上下文丰富任务中，虽然能遵循主要推理路径，但会遗漏外围、持续性和格式敏感的要求。

Conclusion: LLM的推理失败是有选择性的，并非整体性崩溃，需要针对特定类型要求进行改进。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks reveal that despite strong reasoning capabilities, large language models (LLMs) still struggle to faithfully apply complex contextual knowledge. These failures are often not wholesale reasoning collapses: in context-rich tasks, models may follow the central reasoning path while missing peripheral, persistent, or format-sensitive requirements.

</details>


### [141] [Optimising Factual Consistency in Summarisation via Preference Learning from Multiple Imperfect Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26840)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化训练流程，通过聚合多个弱事实性度量的评分来构建偏好数据集，无需复杂奖励塑造，即可提升文本摘要的事实一致性。实验表明该方法在不同规模的模型上均能带来一致的事实性提升，使小模型达到与大模型相当的事实性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于事实一致摘要的评估指标仍不成熟，单一事实性度量不可靠，限制了其作为强化学习奖励信号来塑造模型行为的有效性。

Method: 1) 聚合多个弱事实性度量的评分；2) 将评分映射为偏好，过滤掉度量间高分歧的案例；3) 针对每篇源文档，通过改变解码策略生成词汇相似的摘要对，使模型从细微词法差异导致的事实差异中学习；4) 仅使用源文档构建高质量偏好数据集。

Result: 从早期编码器-解码器架构到现代大语言模型，均在事实性上取得一致提升；小模型能达到与大模型相当的事实性水平。

Conclusion: 通过聚合多个弱指标而非依赖单一强指标，结合偏好映射和分歧过滤，可以构建有效的训练流程来提升摘要的事实一致性，且该方法具有跨模型架构的普适性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with evaluation metrics as rewards is widely used to enhance specific capabilities of language models. However, for tasks such as factually consistent summarisation, existing metrics remain underdeveloped, limiting their effectiveness as signals for shaping model behaviour.While individual factuality metrics are unreliable, their combination can more effectively capture diverse factual errors. We leverage this insight to introduce an automated training pipeline that improves factual consistency in summaries by aggregating scores from different weak metrics. Our approach avoids the need for complex reward shaping by mapping scores to preferences and filtering out cases with high disagreement between metrics. For each source document, we generate lexically similar summary pairs by varying decoding strategies, enabling the model to learn from factual differences caused by subtle lexical differences. This approach constructs a high-quality preference dataset using only source documents.Experiments demonstrate consistent factuality gains across models, ranging from early encoder-decoder architectures to modern large language models, with smaller models reaching comparable factuality to larger ones.

</details>


### [142] [Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation for Adaptive Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26849)
*Manh Nguyen,Sunil Gupta,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UAB（不确定性感知预算分配）框架，通过每问题的不确定性信号（平均负对数似然）重新分配固定采样预算，用边际贪婪算法求解凹覆盖最大化问题，在不增加推理成本下提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对多个响应均匀采样，导致简单问题过采样而困难问题探索不足，计算资源分配低效。

Method: 两阶段框架：Phase 1对每个问题生成1个响应，提取平均负对数似然（ANLL）作为不确定性/难度信号；Phase 2通过边际贪婪算法求解凹覆盖最大化问题，将剩余预算重新分配给高不确定性（困难）问题，低不确定性（简单）问题分配较少。

Result: 在1.5B~27B的6个开源和黑盒模型、5个推理基准上，UAB平均准确率提升最高+3%，单基准提升最高+5%，低资源场景增益最大，无需额外模型或LLM调用。

Conclusion: 在无额外计算开销下，通过不确定性引导的非均匀预算分配可显著提升多响应采样的推理效率。

Abstract: Sampling multiple responses improves language model reasoning, but uniform compute allocation is inefficient: easy questions are over-sampled while hard questions remain under-explored. We propose Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation (UAB), a concave integer optimization framework that reallocates a fixed sampling budget based on per-question uncertainty estimated at no additional inference cost. In Phase 1, every question receives one generation; its average negative log-likelihood (ANLL), extracted directly from output log-probabilities, serves as a difficulty signal while the generation contributes to the final vote. In Phase 2, the remaining budget is allocated by a marginal-greedy algorithm that solves a concave coverage-maximization surrogate exactly: uncertain questions receive more sampling budget while confident questions receive fewer additional samples. Evaluated on six open-weight and black-box models spanning 1.5B to 27B parameters and five reasoning benchmarks covering math, logic, and preference tasks, UAB outperforms baselines by up to +3% in average accuracy and up to +5% on individual benchmarks, with the largest gains in low-resource settings, requiring no auxiliary model or additional LLM call. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/manhitv/UAB.

</details>


### [143] [GeoFaith: A Spatio-Temporal Dual View of Faithful Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26893)
*Weijiang Lv,Wentong Zhao,Jiayu Wang,Yuhao Wu,Jiaheng Wei,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GeoFaith框架，利用潜在几何结构和熵动力学来诊断和强制思维链推理的忠实性，通过可扩展的引导流水线将步级标注从1k扩展到20k样本，训练8B忠实性检测器，并设计忠实性感知的强化学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果监督的思维链推理存在事后合理化问题，产生看似合理但不忠实的推理链，且大多数忠实性评估方法不可扩展、昂贵或不可靠。

Method: 提出GeoFaith时空框架，利用潜在几何结构和熵动力学诊断并强制忠实推理；开发可扩展的引导流水线，将步级标注从1k扩展到20k样本；训练8B参数忠实性检测器；设计忠实性感知的强化学习框架，联合优化结果正确性、过程忠实性和轨迹一致性。

Result: 在忠实性检测和下游推理任务上均取得优越性能，生成的推理链更短、更可解释且不牺牲准确性。

Conclusion: GeoFaith有效解决了思维链推理的忠实性问题，兼顾了检测能力和推理质量。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has advanced large language models (LLMs), but outcome-based supervision leads to pervasive post-hoc rationalization, producing plausible yet unfaithful reasoning chains. Most prior faithfulness assessment methods are either unscalable, expensive, or unreliable. We propose GeoFaith, a spatio-temporal framework that leverages latent geometric structure and entropy dynamics to diagnose and enforce faithful reasoning. We develop a scalable bootstrapping pipeline expanding step-level annotations from 1k to 20k samples across four domains, train an 8B faithfulness detector outperforming GPT-5 on standard benchmarks, and design a faithfulness-aware reinforcement learning framework jointly optimizing outcome correctness, process faithfulness, and trajectory consistency. Experiments show the proposed method achieves superior performance on both faithfulness detection and downstream reasoning, producing shorter, more interpretable chains without sacrificing accuracy. Our code will be made available publicly.

</details>


### [144] [Are Video Models Zero-Shot Learners and Reasoners in Education? EduVideoBench, A Knowledge-Skills-Attitude Benchmark for Educational Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26918)
*Unggi Lee,Hoyoung Ahn,Yoon Choi,Seonmin Eun,Jahyun Jeong,Seonmin Jin,Harmony Jung,Hye Jin Kim,Chaerin Lee,Hyunji Lee,Jeongjin Lee,Soohwan Lee,Young-Seok Oh,Jaehyeon Park,Sun-ok Ryu,Sunyoung Shin,Yoorim Son,Haeun Park,Yeil Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EduVideoBench是首个基于KSA框架的教育视频生成基准，从知识、技能、态度三个维度评估教学有效性和安全性。实验表明现有VGMs在课堂就绪度上存在显著不足，单个元素错位（如节奏、可读性）即可导致视频无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评测仅关注感知质量、忠实性、通用安全或推理能力，缺乏对教育场景下教学有效性和安全性的评估。

Method: 基于知识-技能-态度（KSA）框架构建教育领域平衡基准EduVideoBench，对五个前沿视频生成模型进行多维度评测，并结合专家评论进行定性分析。

Result: 五个前沿VGMs在知识、技能、态度维度均存在较大改进空间，尚未达到课堂就绪水平；教育有效性是多组件耦合的，一个元素（节奏、可读性、符号）错位即可使视频失效。

Conclusion: EduVideoBench可为发展教学合理且课堂安全的视频生成模型提供指导。

Abstract: Video generation models (VGMs) are rapidly entering classrooms, yet existing benchmarks evaluate only perceptual quality, intrinsic faithfulness, generic safety, or video as a reasoning medium, and none assesses whether the outputs are educationally valid. In this work, we present EduVideoBench, the first balanced benchmark in the education domain, grounded in the Knowledge-Skills-Attitude (KSA) framework so that pedagogical adequacy and educational safety are evaluated jointly rather than as ad-hoc quality dimensions. Across five frontier VGMs, our results show substantial room for improvement across knowledge, skills, and attitude before they are classroom-ready. We complement this with a qualitative analysis of expert comments, finding that educational validity is multi-component, where a single misaligned element such as pacing, legibility, or notation can invalidate an otherwise correct video. We hope EduVideoBench will guide the development of VGMs that are pedagogically grounded and safe for the classroom.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Adapt SFT Data for Better Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26924)
*Lisong Sun,Li Wang,Chen Zhang,Jinyang Wu,Kui Zhang,Tianhao Peng,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DART提出一种基于强化学习的数据适配方法，通过训练映射模型将分布不匹配的SFT数据转换为更适配目标模型分布的监督信号，从而提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用专家数据对LLM进行SFT微调时，若数据分布与目标模型自身分布不匹配，会损害泛化能力。需解决固定SFT数据集与目标模型之间的分布对齐问题。

Method: DART将分布不匹配的SFT数据集使用转化为一个关于示范变换的优化问题，通过强化学习训练一个映射模型（mapper model），将原始SFT数据转换为更匹配目标模型分布和学习偏好的适配监督，再用变换后的数据进行SFT微调。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，DART提升了泛化能力，相比直接RL训练具有更高的训练效率，并帮助模型超越标准SFT的性能。

Conclusion: DART通过数据适配有效解决了SFT中数据分布不匹配问题，是一种高效且通用的推理微调后训练方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, with post-training playing a crucial role in enhancing their reasoning capabilities. Among post-training paradigms, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely used: it leverages external data to provide dense supervision and enables efficient training. However, directly fine-tuning on expert data can hurt generalization when the data distribution is mismatched with the target model's own distribution. In this work, we propose Data Adaptation for Reasoning Tuning (DART), which formulates the use of a fixed, potentially distributionally misaligned SFT dataset as an optimization problem over demonstration transformations. DART trains a mapper model with reinforcement learning to convert original SFT data into model-adapted supervision that better matches the target model's distribution and learning preferences. The transformed data are then used for SFT, allowing the target model to better exploit external supervision. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show that DART improves generalization, achieves higher training efficiency than direct RL, and helps models surpass standard SFT. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DART525E50D.

</details>


### [146] [Reasoning Depth and Environment Complexity: A Controlled Study of RLVR Data Allocation across Logical Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26934)
*Yihua Zhu,Qianying Liu,Fei Cheng,Jiaxin Wang,Akiko Aizawa,Sadao Kurohashi,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有RLVR研究将推理空间狭隘地视为推理深度单一维度，本文提出难度（深度+环境复杂度）和奖励推理形式（演绎、溯因、归纳、类比）二维表征，通过合成知识图谱环境实验发现：联合覆盖优于单轴训练、各推理家族响应不均（溯因推理在RL覆盖区域外退化）、均匀混合优于阶段式课程，且商业模型也存在演绎-溯因不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR研究对推理空间的定义过于狭隘，只关注推理深度和演绎推理，忽略了真实世界中多维度的推理难度（如环境复杂度）和多种推理形式（溯因、归纳、类比等），导致模型在更广泛的推理场景中存在能力缺陷。

Method: 构建合成知识图谱环境，沿两个维度（难度：深度+复杂度；奖励推理形式：演绎、溯因、归纳、类比）系统控制预训练和后训练数据分布，通过对比实验分析不同训练策略的效果。

Result: 1）联合深度-复杂度覆盖优于单轴训练；2）推理家族响应非均匀，溯因推理在RL覆盖区域外退化，任务相关性聚类为演绎-溯因和归纳-类比两对；3）在固定预算下，均匀混合优于阶段式课程；4）商业模型同样存在演绎-溯因不对称性。

Conclusion: RLVR训练需在难度维度（深度+复杂度）和推理形式维度（多类型推理能力）上同时覆盖，才能提升模型在多样化推理任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become central to post-training reasoning models, yet a key limitation of existing studies is their narrow view of the reasoning space: difficulty is treated as reasoning depth alone, and reward is concentrated on forward deductive state tracking. We instead characterize the reasoning space along two dimensions. Difficulty. Beyond reasoning depth, we study environment complexity, where models must identify the correct path amid distractors and interacting structures. Rewarded reasoning form. We consider four abilities core to real-world reasoning: deductive state tracking, abductive recovery of hidden events or facts, inductive rule induction, and analogical transfer. To disentangle these factors, we construct a synthetic knowledge-graph environment with controlled pre- and post-training distributions, where each instance varies along depth, complexity, and task family. Three findings emerge: joint depth-complexity coverage outperforms single-axis recipes; reasoning families respond non-uniformly, with abductive reasoning degrading outside the RL-covered region and task correlations clustering into deductive-abductive and inductive-analogy pairs; and uniform mixing outperforms staged curricula under a fixed budget. We also find that recent off-the-shelf models exhibit the same deductive-over-abductive asymmetry, suggesting that this gap is not merely an artifact of our controlled setup.

</details>


### [147] [DunbaaBERT: From Sacrifice to Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26935)
*Iffat Maab,Waleed Jamil,Raphael Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DunbaaBERT是一个从零训练的Urdu RoBERTa-base模型家族，使用三种不同词表大小（32k、52k、96k）的Byte-BPE分词器在17GB Urdu语料上训练。在词法接受性、新闻分类、冒犯性语言检测和情感分析等任务上，模型在保持高效性的同时达到与强多语言基线可比的性能，其中32k词表变体提供了最佳效率轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Urdu语言在NLP领域由于资源有限和评估环境碎片化而未被充分探索，现有大语言模型对Urdu支持不足，缺乏专门针对Urdu的预训练编码器模型。

Method: 从零训练Urdu RoBERTa-base模型家族DunbaaBERT，使用Byte-BPE分词器构建32k、52k、96k三种词表大小，在去重后的17GB Urdu语料上进行预训练，并在词法接受性、新闻分类、冒犯性语言检测和情感分析等任务上进行评估。

Result: DunbaaBERT变体在多项基准测试中达到与强多语言基线可比的性能，同时在效率方面保持优势。较大词表并未持续提升下游任务效果，32k词表的DunbaaBERT提供了最佳整体效率轮廓。

Conclusion: 精心策划的Urdu专用编码器模型即使在相对紧凑的模型和训练规模下也能保持高度竞争力，词表大小需权衡性能与效率，并非越大越好。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong performance across many NLP tasks, yet Urdu remains comparatively underexplored due to limited resources and fragmented evaluation settings. To address this gap, we introduce DunbaaBERT, a family of Urdu RoBERTa-base models trained from scratch with Byte-BPE vocabularies of 32k, 52k, and 96k tokens on a deduplicated 17GB Urdu corpus. We evaluate DunbaaBERT across intrinsic and downstream Urdu NLP benchmarks covering linguistic acceptability, news classification, offensive language detection, and sentiment analysis while analyzing vocabulary-size effects on performance and efficiency trade-offs. Across benchmarks, the DunbaaBERT variants achieve competitive performance against strong multilingual baselines while consistently maintaining favorable efficiency trade-offs. Interestingly, larger vocabularies do not consistently improve downstream effectiveness, with DunbaaBERT$_{\text{32k}}$ repeatedly providing the strongest overall efficiency profile. Overall, our results demonstrate that carefully curated Urdu-specific encoder models can remain highly competitive despite comparatively compact model and training scales. All models are released under the MIT license.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Questions: Evaluating What Large Language Models (Actually) Know](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26937)
*Luca Giordano,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出开放知识评估范式，通过开放式提示（如"告诉我关于X的一切"）而非预设问题来评估LLM的 parametric knowledge，并发布了包含10,000个实体的BeQu基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识基准依赖预设问题，存在可及性偏差（availability bias），只能评估设计者选择查询的知识，无法全面衡量模型实际具备的 parametric knowledge。

Method: 提出开放知识评估范式：使用开放式引导提示（如"告诉我关于X的一切"）让模型自由展示知识；构建BeQu基准（10,000个实体+参考语料库用于陈述验证）；评估多种LLM并分析推理努力、模型规模、提示格式和知识领域的影响。

Result: 建立了BeQu基准（10,000个实体+参考语料），评估了多种语言模型，分析了推理努力、模型规模、提示格式和知识领域等因素对知识表达的影响。

Conclusion: 开放知识评估范式和BeQu基准为更全面地理解LLM的 parametric knowledge 提供了新方法，超越了传统基于预设问题的评估方式。

Abstract: Parametric knowledge in large language models (LLMs) is a cornerstone of their success, yet remains poorly understood. Existing knowledge benchmarks typically rely on predefined questions (e.g., "What is the birth date of M.L. King?"), evaluating only knowledge that benchmark designers explicitly choose to query, a problematic availability bias.
  In this paper, we introduce open knowledge evaluation, a new paradigm for LLM knowledge benchmarking. Instead of asking narrow questions, it evaluates models on the knowledge they choose to surface in response to open-ended elicitation prompts (e.g., "Tell me everything you know about M.L. King"). This shifts the focus from predefined answer retrieval toward characterizing the knowledge models naturally express.
  We instantiate this paradigm with BeQu (Beyond Questions), a benchmark of 10,000 entities paired with reference corpora for statement verification. Using BeQu, we evaluate a broad range of language models and analyze the effects of reasoning effort, model scale, prompt format, and knowledge domain. Data and leaderboard are available on this work's GitHub repository and at the benchmark's website.

</details>


### [149] [Accountable Human-AI Deliberation with LLMs: Scaling Collective Intelligence through Symbiotic Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26940)
*Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种共生式人机协作框架，通过三层结构（观察与多样性放大、带条款级来源的促进、人类最终批准）实现大规模民主 deliberation，在保持集体智能扩展的同时确保参与者的自主性和合法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯LLM中介会削弱多元性、过度优化一致性、且在参与者无法质疑自身被表征方式时损害合法性，因此需要一种既能扩展集体智能又能保留人类能动性的共生框架。

Method: 提出三层框架：1) 观察与多样性放大层；2) 带条款级来源追溯的促进层；3) 人类优先批准层。贡献包括：分级覆盖率、多样性和擦除指标（含显著性加权）；结合交叉编码器相似性与因果剔除诊断的来源追溯管线；偏好条件化的权衡控制；公平感知的可争议工作流；对抗鲁棒性测试；以及基于LLM作为评判者局限性的消融实验评估协议。

Result: 提供了一个可测试的蓝图，用于构建既能扩展集体智能又能保留人类能动性和合法性的 deliberation 技术。

Conclusion: 共生式人机框架在理论上平衡了LLM的扩展能力与人类的核心决策权，通过多层设计保障了民主 deliberation 中的多元性、可追溯性和合法性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support democratic deliberation at scales previously constrained by turn-taking and facilitation bandwidth. Recent work shows that LLM-generated group statements are often preferred over human-mediated outputs, while theoretical analyses argue that LLMs relax the simultaneity constraints limiting collective intelligence.
